Do not run before the experiments and revision are actually done. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Molecular-Cell-Skills/skills/molcell-rebuttal) · ★1.1k)

Typical phrases include "let's design X", "shape this into a spec", "what should the API for Z look like", and "/mold".
